# Gap register — every claim with its evidence

The evidence appendix for [ide-gap-analysis.md](ide-gap-analysis.md). One row per open item, 735 of
them, each carrying the file, symbol or empty grep that establishes the state it claims. Read the
analysis for the verdict and the priorities; read this when you want to know whether a particular
line is true, or when you are about to start work on one and need the entry point.

Every row was produced by an agent that was required to check the repository before calling
something missing, on the rule that a false gap — "you don't have X" when we do — costs more than a
missed one. Where the evidence is an absence, it names the grep that came back empty.

The same caveats as the analysis apply: de-duplication is approximate (±5%), impact is a judgement,
`out-of-scope` is a decision rather than a fact, and `blocked-by-client` was reserved for the 11
cases where the protocol or host genuinely cannot express the capability.

Domains are ordered by high-impact count, same as the analysis.
